## Austria — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Austria: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): █▇▅▆▅▅▄▃▃▃▁▅ (min -17.0, max -12.7).

## Belgium — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Belgium: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▁▄▅▆▅▆▆▇▇██ (min -42.8, max -30.3).

## Finland — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Finland: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): █▇▇▇▇▆▅▄▄▄▃▁ (min -21.1, max -15.2).

## France — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

France: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): █▆▄▂▂▁▁▂▂▂▂▂ (min -17.3, max -8.5).

## Hungary — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Hungary: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. ## Italy — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Italy: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▃▁▂▁▂▁▃▄▅▆▇█ (min -19.8, max -13.6).

## Luxembourg — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Luxembourg: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. ## Netherlands — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Netherlands: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▃▃▁▁▂▂▂▂▄▆█ (min -55.0, max -47.2).

## Norway — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Norway: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Russia: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. ## United Kingdom — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

United Kingdom: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▂▃▆▇█▇▂▂▅▇▅ (min -22.8, max -18.3).
